  - **Full-Body 3D Solid Ice Block Encasements, `Frost Nova Burst`, `Flash-Freeze Stasis`, `Glacial Spikes Retaliation` & `Thermal Shock`**: Enemies frozen solid are now encased inside a stationary, full-body 3D crystal block of ice (without suffocating inside solid blocks) that completely stops movement, aiming, bow drawing, and attacks before shattering cleanly into ice shards—or exploding early in a **Thermal Shock** steam blast when hit by **Nether** fire. **Frost Nova Burst** erupts a lingering `6`-block ring of jagged 3D ice spikes that blasts foes backward across newly frozen ground before locking grounded enemies inside 3D ice blocks for `2` seconds; **Flash-Freeze Stasis** converts weapon swings into **Frozen Materia** damage and locks your main target in 3D ice for `1.5` seconds (`0.75` seconds on nearby cleave targets); and **Glacial Spikes Retaliation** surrounds you (or anchored allies) with 8 outward-pointing 3D ice spikes (`50%` damage from Water, `150%` from Fire/Nether/Undead, Powder Snow freeze immunity) that stab melee attackers and cut both player and monster swing speed by `30%`.
  - **Non-Melting `Rime Pitfall`, `10m Hoarfrost Flash-Freeze` & Cave-Ready `Colossal Glacial Monolith`**: **Rime Pitfall** arms in `1` second (`10`-minute or Tier 5+ indefinite lifetime, restoring original ground cleanly if disarmed) and turns a `3x3` patch of floor into hairline-cracked frosted ice that never melts into messy water—collapsing when stepped on to drop mobs into a `3`-block-deep pit; **Hoarfrost Flash-Freeze** sweeps a full `10`-block, `60°` cone that freezes standing and flowing water and lava into solid walkable causeways and dusts dry ground in temporary frost; and **Colossal Glacial Monolith** opens an overhead snowflake rift (adapting cleanly to indoor cave ceilings) that drops a giant 3D spire of **Blue Ice** after `2` seconds, crushing the area and leaving behind a permanent **Blue Ice** monument.
  - **3D `Glacial Eidolon` Summon Triad (`#98DCF2`), 3D Player Statue `Frostwalker Stasis`, Leak-Sealing `Silk Touch & Permafrost` & `Permafrost Stasis Hex`**: **Glacial Eidolon Agents** freeze foes solid in 3D ice blocks on every 3rd hit; **Glacial Icicle Sentries** fire fast, straight icicle darts with crisp crystal audio; and **Glacial Ice Effigies** shatter into a `4`-block **Frost Nova** that freezes water and locks nearby attackers in 3D ice blocks. **Frostwalker Stasis** teleports you onto safe ice pads while leaving behind a taunting 3D ice statue copy of yourself wearing your gear inside an ice prism; **Silk Touch & Permafrost** mines blocks with guaranteed **Silk Touch**, automatically freezes exposed water and lava leaks into ice and obsidian while mining, compacts snow into packed ice, and freezes dropped water/lava buckets; and **Permafrost Stasis Hex** locks sprinting every tick, cuts speed by `60%`, and boosts physical shatter damage taken by `+50%` until thawed by fire or Restoration magic.

  - **High-Definition Spell Rune Icons & Tabletop Art**: Replaced all blocky low-resolution pixel runes and table graphics with crisp, glowing, smooth-edged symbols and detailed tabletop artwork (parchment scroll, wooden rollers, astrological silver-gold & brass rings, golden 12-o'clock needle, glass Materia inkwells, wax seals, Materia vials, and Mutation switch) with clean, non-overlapping button labels and golden tooltips.
- **Trigger Logic Ring, Void-Warp Travel Mutation Gate & Point-of-Contact Multi-Cast (`I`–`VIII`)**:
  - **8 Trigger Conditions on the Outer Ring**: Separated **Trigger Logic** from **Flow** so any trigger can be paired with any travel pattern: **On-Entity-Hit** (triggers on mobs, or splashes at `75%` power if it misses into terrain), **On-Block-Collision** (skips past mobs to detonate at `100%` power on floors, walls, or ceilings), **Timed Fuse** (`0.5s–3.0s` right-click dial; splits mid-air when paired with **Bifurcation**, **Trifurcation**, or **Omnication Dome**), **On-Proximity** (`3`-block hostile sensor orb), **On-Release** (hold right-click to compress and release to fire, gaining up to `+15%` bonus power over `1.0s`), **Corpse Bloom** (marks a struck foe for `3` seconds and bursts out of its corpse on death), **Self**, and **Self-Timed** (`30s / 60s / 120s` right-click dial).
  - **Void-Warp Travel Mutation Gate**: **Void-Warp Relay** now requires **Void Materia** or a Materia created using Void (**Astral, Null-R, Null-U, Wraith, Penumbra, Abyss, Oblivion, Entropic, Cataclysm, Eschaton, Entropica**, or **Singularity**) infused into the **Flow Ring** with **Mutation: ACTIVE**—locking and dimming the rune when non-Void Materia is selected, and automatically resetting back to **Normal Flow** if Void-lineage Materia or the Flow Mutation switch is turned off.
  - **True Point-of-Contact Multi-Cast (`I`–`VIII`) & Pure Flow Modifiers**: Separated **Single Cast (`1x`) through Octuple Cast (`8x`)** in the Left Drawer from the **9 pure Flow Modifiers** (**Normal Flow**, **Bifurcation**, **Trifurcation**, **Omnication Dome**, **Leaping Chain**, **Satellite Orbit**, **Accelerated Fast**, **Deliberate Slow**, and **Void-Warp Relay**) so every payload casts all `1–8` slotted spells right at its hit spot, while missed `25% / 75%` triggered shots refund their upfront Materia fuel back into your **Orbis Cell** after 5 seconds.
- **3D Spell Visuals Overhaul & Complete `#1 Air` Materia Spell Suite**:
  - **True 3D Spell Rendering & Custom Materia Colors**: Replaced flat vanilla particle puffs with glowing 3D **Beams**, **Crescent Slashes**, **Swirling Cones**, **Expanding Shockwave Rings**, **Spinning Tornado Pillars**, **Rotating Ground Sigils**, **Teleport Rifts**, custom **3D Projectiles** with ribbon trails, and multi-part **3D Eidolon Constructs** tinted to your spell's exact Materia colors.
  - **Majestic `#1 Air` Visuals & Pure Materia Combat**: Fixed spells so `100%` of Combat **Harm** runs through pure **Materia** advantage/weakness math (`16–2,500 HP` across Tiers 1–10), added `30s` melee weapon priming + `50%` cleave splash on **Touch Strike**, added `50%` fuel-refunding **Sneak + Cast** disarming on **Glyphs**, and gave every `#1 Air` spell custom golden-buff & silver-ivory 3D tempest visuals—including **Turbulence Spiral** swept-fin gale lances with twin supersonic vapor cones and corkscrew wakes, **Sonic Leap Chains** arching wind-bridges, **Jetstream Propulsion** beams with Mach shock-diamonds and racing wind rings, **Gust Shield** spheres orbited by razor gale-crescents, **Concussive Uppercut** triple-blade cleaves & flared cyclone geysers, **Slipstream Shield** supersonic sprinting bow-waves, glowing 8-point Wind-Rose **Geyser Mines**, full-body **Howling Gale** hurricane funnels, towering 14-tier volumetric **Tornado Vortex** supercells, inward-spiraling **Vacuum Implosion** blinks, breath-draining **Hypoxia** vortex collars, and winged/wind-chime/cyclone **Zephyr Eidolons**.
  - **Persistent 3D Ward & Aegis Barrier Shells with Hit-Flare Opacity**: Active **Arcane Wards** and **Aegis / Elemental Barrier Mantles** now stay continuously visible around protected players, allies, and Eidolons for their entire duration instead of fading after a brief flash—wrapping the wearer in a smooth, camera-friendly 3D dome, rotating runic hoops, ground sigil, and orbiting hexagonal shield plates that **flare brightly in opacity with directional shockwave ripples** whenever they block or absorb a hit, and burst outward cleanly when shattered.
- **Continuous Hold-Right-Click Beams (`Materia/s`), Next-Hit Touch/Strike Priming & Flying Air Wind-Blade Projectiles**:
  - **Continuous Hold-Right-Click Beams (`Materia/s` Fuel Drain)**: **Beam** spells now channel continuously straight from your hand toward wherever you aim while holding **Right-Click** instead of firing a carrier orb that summons a beam from above. Beams display and drain **`Materia/second`** (`5` smooth damage/utility pulses per second, flicker-free 3D beam rendering, full Flow support for Twin/Triple/Dome/Orbit/Chain/Void-Warp beams from the caster, and an automatic **Burst Finisher** shockwave pulse every `3` seconds of continuous hold) and stop immediately when you let go of Right-Click or run out of Materia.
  - **Next-Hit `Touch / Strike` Weapon & Hand Priming**: Casting any **Touch / Strike** spell (`1x` Single Cast through `8x` Octuple Cast) now always charges up your weapon or hand for `30` seconds instead of launching a spell projectile—unleashing all slotted Touch effects the next time you physically hit an enemy (`100%` power on your target + `50%` sweeping cleave splash + held weapon Materia Advantage bonus) or punch a block for Utility Touch spells. Holding a charge past `30` seconds gently sips `10%` Materia/second until used, or you can **Sneak + Right-Click** to safely put the charge away for free.
  - **Flying Crescent Wind-Blade Projectiles for `#1 Air` Harm**: **Air** `Harm` projectiles now fly forward as a sweeping 3-layer golden-buff & radiant-ivory **3D Crescent Wind-Blade**—a flying version of the `Touch / Strike` crescent slash with a white-hot razor cutting edge and twin wingtip wind trails.
- **Guide-Accurate `Blink / Step` Overhaul & `#1 Air` Departure `Vacuum Implosion`**:
  - **Instant `16m+` Raycast Step, Safe Ground Check & `100%` Materia Refund**: Casting a **Blink / Step** spell now teleports you **instantly** up to **16 blocks** along your crosshair (`16m` at Tiers 1–3 with line-of-sight; scaling further and **phasing through solid walls, cliffs, and doors at Tier 4+ or Void infusion**) instead of waiting on a slow projectile orb or jumping across nearby mobs. Blink steps backward along your aim line to snap safely onto **solid, hazard-free ground** (refusing lava, fire, magma, powder snow, cacti, and spikes), converts downward freefall speed into forward horizontal speed, stays completely **silent and smoke-free by default** unless mutated or paired with an arrival spell, and **cancels safely with a `100%` Materia fuel refund** if no safe landing spot exists.
  - **`Self-Blink` vs. `Position Swap` Toggle at the Scribing Table**: Right-click the **Blink** rune (or its Left Drawer focus pill) at the **Arcane Scribing Table** to toggle between **Self-Blink** and **Position Swap** (`SWAP` badge), letting you trade places with a targeted enemy in combat.
  - **`#1 Air` `Vacuum Implosion` Departure Suction-Pop**: Infusing **Air Materia** into **Blink** (with **Mutation: ON**) leaves a collapsing golden-ivory **Vacuum Implosion** right at the spot you blinked away from—pulling nearby enemies, loose items, and enemy arrows inward for `0.5` seconds into a tight cluster before finishing with a crisp sonic **Vacuum Pop**.
- **Outer Rim Spell Travel Distance Circle (`0m` to `128m`), Range Cost Scaling & Tactile Stone Grinding Audio**:
  - **New Outermost Rotating Distance Circle on the Arcane Scribing Table**: Added a 9-stop astrological **Travel Distance Circle** around the outer rim of the spell wheel with stops at **`0m`**, **`8m`**, **`16m`** *(default)*, **`24m`**, **`32m`**, **`48m`**, **`64m`**, **`96m`**, and **`128m`**. Drag, scroll, or click any stop to line it up under the golden 12 o'clock needle, or **Shift + Right-Click** the outer rim to snap straight back to `16m`.
  - **Light Stone Grinding Sound While Turning Spell Circles**: Dragging, scrolling, or clicking to spin any of the **Arcane Scribing Table**'s concentric circles now plays a soft, tactile carved-stone sliding and notch-settling sound (with deeper stone tones on the large outer rings and lighter tones on the inner rings).
  - **True Travel Distance Control Across Spells & Materia Cost Modifier**: Your chosen distance stop directly sets how far your spells travel—controlling **Projectile & Multi-Cast carrier flight distance**, **Beam reach**, **Blink & Position Swap distance**, **Void-Warp range**, **Grapple reach**, and **ranged Glyph / Skyfall / Summon placement** (without shrinking or bloating explosion sizes). Picking `0m` fires immediately at point-blank range for a **`25%` Materia discount** (`0.75x`), `8m` grants a **`15%` discount** (`0.85x`), `16m` stays at **`1.00x` standard cost**, and longer sniper distances scale Materia cost smoothly up to **`3.40x` at `128m`**.
- **New `Entropica: Spellgems` Creative Testing Tab & Instant Creative Gem Swapping**:
  - **Dedicated Spellgem Testing Tab**: Added a new **Entropica: Spellgems** Creative tab (`spellgems_tab`) stocked with casting gear on the top row (**Arcanum Focus**, **Wand**, **Staff**, **Grimoire**, **Blank Spellgem**, **Arcane Scribing Table**, **Runic Anvil**, and **Autocast Pedestals**) followed by ready-to-cast **Inscribed Spellgems** for every **Materia** type—covering all **11 Delivery Forms** (including all 3 Eidolon summon types), all **Flow Modifiers & Delivery Vectors** (including **Void-Warp** on Void-lineage Materia, **Multi-Cast**, and **Dual-Verb**), all **Trigger Modes**, and all **19 Core Effects**—with every gem locked to a single Materia type and set to standard `16m` length.
  - **Instant Creative Mode Inventory Socketing & Live Color-Shifting Gem Icons**: Right-clicking a **Spellgem** onto a **Focus**, **Wand**, **Staff**, or **Grimoire** inside your inventory while in **Creative Mode** now sockets or swaps it immediately with **0-second wait and 0% backfire**, and dynamic/multi-color Materia Spellgems now shimmer with their live colors right in the Creative tab and apparatus sockets.

## Build 000-1-26-315

- **Scholar's Journals (Volumes I–XIII) & Interactive Dual-Page Journal Reader**:
  - Added **13 collectible Scholar's Journal items** (`celestial_journal_vol1` – `celestial_journal_vol13`), bringing all **233 lore chapters, memoirs, war diaries, and field entries** from the Entropica Obsidian Wiki directly into the game: **Vol. I** (*The Shattered Dome*), **Vol. II** (*Tales of the Fifty Firmaments*), **Vol. III** (*Brass, Glass, and Starlight*), **Vol. IV** (*The Thread of Starlight*), **Vol. V** (*The City Under the Dome*), **Vol. VI** (*The Broken Loom*), **Vol. VII** (*The Wanderers of the Outer Dark*), **Vol. VIII** (*When Stars Shatter*), **Vol. IX** (*The Deep Sky Tapestry*), **Vol. X** (*The Sovereigns of the Firmament*), **Vol. XI** (*The Celestial Artisans*), **Vol. XII** (*Horrors of the Crimson Abyss*), and **Vol. XIII** (*The Starlight Covenant*).
  - **13 Custom Pixel-Art Journal Covers**: Each volume has its own unique 32x32 leather binding color, corner trim, fore-edge strap clasp, silk bookmark ribbon, and cover emblem matching its story.
  - **Dual-Page Open Journal Screen**: Right-clicking any journal opens a warm two-page parchment book with a clickable chapter list on the left, smooth scrolling story text on the right (with epigraph quotes, bold/italic styling, and boxed formulas), arrow-key page flipping, and saved bookmarks that remember which chapters you've read and where you left off.
  - **Obtaining & Storage**: Available in Creative tabs, craftable in Survival from a Book + Scribing Ink (or Ink Sac) + two themed ingredients per volume, found in structure chests (Stronghold Libraries, Ancient Cities, Mansions, Temples, Dungeons, Bastions, End Cities, and Trial Vaults), dropped by Gemini Goat Mages, Evokers, Witches, Cosmic mobs, and Bosses, and storable inside **Chiseled Bookshelves**.

- **Full Guide-Complete Spellcrafting Engine & All 58 Materia Spell Suites (#1 Air – #57 Entropica + Chimera)**:
  - **Eidolon Summon Creation Forms (Choose Autonomous Agent, Anchored Sentry, or Phantom Decoy Per Spell Across All 58 Materia Suites)**:
    - Split summon creation into **3 separate Delivery Form choices**—**Summon: Autonomous Agent** (`summon_agent`), **Summon: Anchored Sentry** (`summon_sentry`), and **Summon: Phantom Decoy** (`summon_decoy`)—so players pick **one** summon type per spell, and each spell displays the exact construct name for that chosen summon across all 58 canonical Materia suites (`#1 Air` through `#57 Entropica` + `Chimera`), complete with smooth color-shifting skins: **Autonomous Agents** (mobile companions up to `140 HP`, including hovering **Solar Seraph** archers, indoor ceiling-ambushing **Umbral** stalkers, crop-harvesting & replanting **Spore-Tenders**, piercing-lance **Aurora** gliders, twin-afterimage **Mirage-Duelists**, wall-phasing **Wraith/Astral/Null** hunters, random 4-element **Chimera** beasts, flying **Prismatic Valkyries**, burrowing **Stygian Magma-Void Serpents**, **Event-Horizon Leviathans**, **Celestial Seraph-Titans**, and alternating Void/Radiant-orb **Avatars of Entropica**), **Anchored Sentries** (stationary elemental turrets & support spires up to `150 HP`, including 16-block dark-spawn blocking for **Umbral, Eclipse, Null_U & Penumbra**, crop-growing **Overgrowth, Spore, Oasis, Spring & Sylvan** spires, farmland-hydrating **Oasis** spires, heartbeat-cleansing **Vitae** spires, **Blood** twin-target health-draining pylons, **Aura** `-20%` spell fuel discount spires, **Static** `+6` Armor lightning/fall-immune citadels, **Genesis** 3-target Dawn-Glory spires, **Oblivion** armor-melting obelisks, **Amber** item-protecting vaults, **Prismatic** 12-block `100%` projectile/magic-reflecting mirror monoliths, and 20-block **Singularity** sanctuary/accretion/equilibrium spires), and **Phantom Decoys** (high-threat taunt statues with `4m–20m` death bursts, including equipment-copying **Umbral, Mirage & Aurora** clones, **Prismatic**'s `15s` party-invulnerability dawn burst, and **Eschaton**'s block-to-fuel micro-nova). High-tier constructs (`#40–#57` + `Chimera`) run automated **45s / 60s / 90s refresh timers** (`12s / 14s / 16s / 20s` for volatile Decoys).
    - **Field Controls**: **Sneak + Right-Click** an Eidolon with a matching **Orbis Cell** to heal it in the field, or **Sneak + Cast** your summon spell to safely dismiss active Eidolons matching that spell's chosen summon type at **0 Materia cost**.
  - **Upgraded Core Spell Verbs**:
    - **Harm**: Tier 1–3 spells respect physical armor while Tier 4+ spells pierce straight through armor, radial blasts scale smoothly from `100%` power at the center down to `33%` at the edge, and heavy overkill kills burst into collectible **Raw Essence** drops.
    - **Kinetic**: Added **Push**, **Pull**, **Launch**, and **Slam** modes, mid-air arrow/boat/minecart/item sweeping, self-rocket-jumps, and bonus blunt damage against knockback-immune bosses.
    - **Arcane Ward**: Scales to `1.5x` base spell damage (`24 HP` at Tier 1 up to `3,750 HP` at Tier 10), ignores non-combat fall/hunger damage, blocks all overflow damage on the hit that breaks the shield, and refills `25%` of blocked matching-element damage back into your **Orbis Cell**.
    - **Blink**: Silent stealth transit by default, line-of-sight wall rules for Tiers 1–3 vs. wall-phasing at Tier 4+, smart backward wall-snapping, freefall-to-forward speed conversion, **Swap** position trading, and a `+50%` **Spatial Fatigue** fuel surcharge when chain-blinking within 3 seconds.
    - **Transmute, Persistent Hexes, Restoration & Conjure**: Added dropped-item transmutation (with `30%` fuel refunds on stacks of 16+ and charcoal charring for invalid plants/wood), **Level I–III stacking Indefinite Hexes** (with elemental counter-cleansing, natural health regen locks, caster-death dispel, and undead/void/predator mob lures), **Burst vs. Mending Flow** healing (with undead Materia damage inversion, `100%` summon healing, and full-health hunger/speed/resistance buffs), and **Conjure** brightness dials (`7–15`) plus 8-second glass/ice stepping bridges.

  - **Physical Spellcrafting Workstations, Consumables & Custom 3D Models**:
    - **Arcane Scribing Table**: Upgraded the 2-block-wide drafting workstation with a top-down interactive tabletop screen: **drag out the Left Brass Drawer** to stamp **Combat / Utility / Misc** wax seals, switch **Sub-Spell tabs (`I–VIII`)**, pick colored **Materia Vials & Shards**, and flip the physical **Mutation (`OFF / ON`)** lever; **drag or scroll the concentric parchment rune rings** (**Flow**, **Delivery Form**, **Core Effect**, and **Dual-Verb Core**) to line up symbols under the golden 12 o'clock needle (including purple `?` runes for blind **Cryptic** testing) with rich **Spell Decision Tooltips** showing power, perks, fuel cost, and cooldown; and **slide open the Right Supply Drawer** to load **Blank Parchment** and **Arcane Scribing Ink**, press the golden **SCRIBE** wax seal with a glowing quill animation, copy blueprints, scrub Spellgems, or safely swap Apparatus sockets.
    - **Runic Anvil & Engraved Weapons**: Added a 2-block-wide anvil for `100%` safe, zero-lockout Spellgem socketing and extraction, **Weapon & Tool Engraving** (adds a right-click spell socket onto swords, axes, pickaxes, shovels, hoes, tridents, or maces), and **Alloy Tempering** (`+5%` to `+30%` spell fuel discounts using Arcanite, Viscanite, Resonite, or Charged Ingots). Placing the two parts onto the anvil (one at a time or both at once) floats them up, spins a glowing **3D Magic Circle** between them, smashes them together with a giant **3D Ethereal Hammer**, and leaves the finished item **floating above the anvil and stored inside the anvil's inventory** until you right-click to pick it up.
    - **Autocast Pedestals (Stone, Marble, Quartz & Astral Slate)**: Added 4 stationary spell-turret pedestals with a `1.0s–60.0s` cast timer slider, `-90°` to `+90°` vertical aim slider, 3 Redstone modes, internal or adjacent **Orbis Cell** fueling, and chest linking (Sneak + Right-Click a container with any Casting Apparatus, then Sneak + Right-Click the pedestal) so mining/farming spells automatically store drops.
    - **Research Bench Spell Study & Blank Spellgem Crafting**: Connected the **Research Bench** (`5s` study timer using Scribing Ink, Blank Parchment, and a Materia sample) to unlock **Observed** spell parts and mutations; consumed **Spell Parchments** on Magic Circle binding; and added both **Blank Spellgem** creation paths (**Starlight Condensation** onto Glass at Astral Collectors/Pedestals/Fountains, and soaking **Spellgem Crystal Seeds** in water for 10 minutes), plus live Materia-tinted 3D crystal rendering across the **Arcanum Focus**, **Wand**, **Staff**, **Grimoire**, **Spellgems**, and workstations.
  - **Custom 3D Spell Projectiles, World Effects & Glowing Materia Particles**:
    - **8 Custom 3D Spell Projectile Shapes & Glowing Ribbon Trails**: Replaced flat 2D item sprites on spell projectiles (and Eidolon turret bolts) with fullbright 3D glowing spell models matched to each Materia type—**Gyroscopic Arcane Bolts**, **Faceted Crystal Lances**, **Spinning Rainbow Chakrams**, **Mini Black Hole Orbs**, **Blazing Solar Crowns**, **Tumbling Molten Meteors**, **Twisting Life-Vine Pods**, and **Crackling Lightning Javelins**—each trailing a smooth 3D glowing ribbon wake plus target-lock rings for homing spells and ghost-shimmer rings for wall-phasing spells.
    - **11 Custom 3D World Spell Effects**: Added full 3D glowing world effects for every spell form, including **spiral-wrapped 3D laser beams**, **branching lightning & chain leaps**, **expanding 3D shockwave domes**, **spinning ground magic circles**, **sweeping melee slash arcs**, **swirling breath funnels**, **sky-to-ground orbital strike pillars**, **vertical teleport rifts**, **hexagonal shield bubbles**, **ritual summon pillars**, and **multi-ring projectile impact bursts**.
    - **42 Glowing Custom Materia & Rune Particles**: Enabled and wired all 18 color-tinted Materia particles (sparks, embers, crystals, arcs, leaves, blood drops, wisps, mist, bubbles, mini-singularities, sunbeams, shields, and sigils) and all 24 glowing Elder Futhark rune particles across both NeoForge and Fabric, complete with smooth fade-in/fade-out glow and spiraling particle trails on casts, impacts, and lingering spell zones.
  - **Crash Fix**: Fixed a right-click item and left-click block crash (including opening the **Entropic Codex**) caused by an outdated Architectury API build version (`13.0.8` -> `18.0.8`).
  - **Arcane Flight, Materia-Colored 3D Wings & Full 19-Spell-Type Overhaul**:
    - **True 3D Flight & Materia-Colored Arcane Wings**: Casting **Arcane Flight** in any spell form now grants real timed 3D flight (`15s` at Tier 1 up to `60s+` at Tier 10), launches you smoothly into the sky, cushions you with **Slow Falling** when it wears off, and unfurls animated **3D Glowing Arcane Wings** on your character's back. Wing colors and feather tips automatically match whichever of the **58 Materia types** you used—including two-tone mixed-Materia spells and live rainbow/cosmic color-shifting wings for **Prismatic**, **Chimera**, **Aurora**, and **Entropica**!
    - **Fixed All Utility, Mobility, Combat & Miscellaneous Spell Effects**: Fixed server-to-client player movement syncing (so **Flight**, **Kinetic** rocket-jumps, **Grapple** hooks, **Blink** momentum, and **Gravity** domes actually move your character smoothly), allowed all non-harmful **Utility** and **Misc** spells (**Ward**, **Grapple**, **Sonar**, **Atmosphere**, **Acoustic**, **Diagnostic**, **Logistics**, and **Gravity**) to fire properly under the Zero-Entity-Harm safety rule, stopped area spells from firing single-shot effects 75 times at once, triggered open-air bolts cleanly at max range, and completed **Grapple** wall/ceiling zip-lines, **Harvest** support for berries/nether wart/cocoa/melons/pumpkins/sugar cane/bamboo, **Logistics** item + XP magnets, **Sonar** ore + creature scanning, **Atmosphere** weather shifting at all tiers, **Acoustic** soundwaves & stealth hush veils, and **Diagnostic** entity/block HUD readouts.
  - Updated the in-game **Entropic Codex** and **Obsidian OKF Wiki**.

## Build 000-1-26-314

- **Modular Spellcrafting Engine & Multi-Materia Spell Parts**:
  - **Mix-and-Match Materia & Per-Part Mutation Switches**: Built the core spellcrafting engine so every individual part of a spell (**Flow**, **Delivery Form**, **Primary Effect**, and optional **Secondary Effect**) can use its own **Materia type** and has its own **Mutation ON/OFF switch**. Turning a part's mutation ON activates that Materia's special behavior, while turning it OFF keeps clean baseline behavior while still using that Materia's color, combat elemental advantage, and fuel cost.
  - **10-Stage Multi-Materia Fueling & Flawed Rituals**: Spells calculate exact fuel costs for each part's Materia type across all 10 Materia stages (**Raw Essence** up to **Liminalia**), pulling fuel cleanly from Orbis Cells, Ampoules, and Raw Essences with zero wasted remainder. Infusing a **Blank Spellgem** with a reusable **Spell Parchment** at a Magic Circle pulls fuel from nearby Orbis Cells within 8 blocks—and if under-supplied, still finishes the gem with a permanent flaw (**Unstable Casting**, a **Crippled Spell Part**, or a **25% chance of both**).
  - **Casting Apparatuses, Socketing & Combat Reactions**:
    - Added functional **Blank Spellgems**, **Spell Parchments**, **Arcanum Focus (1 slot)**, **Arcanist Wand (2 slots)**, **Arcanist Staff (4 slots)**, **Spellweaver Grimoire (9 slots)**, and **Engraved Weapon** casting, plus **Sneak + Scroll Wheel** active spell switching (`0s` swap at workstations vs. `60s` lockout + `45%` first-cast backfire when swapped in the field).
    - Added server-side spell execution, spell projectiles, 2-tick multi-hit combat windows, elemental **Opposing Clashes & Harmonic Mergers**, persistent anti-milk **Hexes** (with 30s boss duration cap and 5-block death spread), blue **Arcane Shields** with friendly-fire protection, safe chest-protected **Utility** mining/harvesting/building, **Codex** two-step spell research + 3-mode server config, and `/entropica spell` testing commands.

## Build 000-1-26-310

- **10-Tier Spell Combat, Fuel, Conversion & Cross-Tier Fueling System**:
  - **10-Stage Materia Conversion Table**: Linked spell tiers directly to the mod's 10-stage Materia conversion ratios (`1 T1 Raw Essence = 8 T2 Fumus`, `1 T3 Sublimata = 360 T2`, `1 T4 Liquida = 6 T3`, `1 T5 Volatilis = 2 T4`, `1 T6 Coagulata = 16 T5`, `1 T7 Ichor = 4 T6`, `1 T8 Transmutata = 5 T7`, `1 T9 Perfecta = 5 T8`, and `1 T10 Liminalia = 5 T9` — equal to **34,560,000 T2 Fumus** or **4,320,000 Raw Essence**).
  - **Orbis Cell Capacities & Down-Conversion Storage**:
    - Every cell tier (**T2 Orbis Cell** through **T10 Athanor Core**) holds **10,000 units of its native Materia stage**.
    - Higher-tier cells can also store lower-tier Materia by converting their 10,000 capacity down using the conversion table (holding much larger amounts of lower-tier fuel), though **a cell can never hold two different Materia stages at the same time**.
  - **Cross-Tier Spell Fueling**:
    - **Higher-Tier Fuel on Lower-Tier Spells**: 1 unit of higher-tier Materia converts into its exact table value of lower-tier fuel with **no bonus and no penalty** (for example, `1 T4 Liquida` pays for `6 units` of `T3 Sublimata` spell casts).
    - **Lower-Tier Fuel on Higher-Tier Spells**: Players can power higher-tier spells using lower-tier Materia by paying the **upward conversion ratio plus a +25% cost markup for each tier jumped**.
  - **Base Cast Fuel & Magic Circle Ritual Costs**:
    - Set base fuel cost per cast across Tiers 1–10: **T1** = `4 Raw Essence`, **T2** = `100 Fumus`, **T3** = `5 Sublimata`, **T4** = `5 Liquida`, **T5** = `5 Volatilis`, **T6** = `2 Coagulata`, **T7** = `2 Ichor`, **T8** = `1 Transmutata`, **T9** = `1 Perfecta`, and **T10** = `1 Liminalia`.
    - Binding a Spellgem at a **Magic Circle** costs **10x the spell's base cast fuel cost**.
  - **Base Combat Damage, Ward Shield HP & Healing Scaling (Tiers 1–10)**:
    - **Base Damage**: Uses the squares of 4 to 8 for Tiers 1–5 (**16, 25, 36, 49, 64 HP**), then scales steeply for Tiers 6–10 (**150, 300, 600, 1,250, 2,500 HP**) to reward the massive cost of refining late-game Materia.
    - **Ward Shield HP (1.5x Base Damage)**: Scales from **24 HP** (Tier 1) up to **3,750 HP** (Tier 10).
    - **Restoration Healing (0.5x Base Damage)**: Scales from **8 HP / 4 Hearts** (Tier 1) up to **1,250 HP / 625 Hearts** (Tier 10).
  - **Delivery Form & Multi-Cast Multipliers**:
    - All **9 Delivery Forms** cost **1.0x Base Fuel** and deal **1.0x Base Power**.
    - **Multi-Cast Spells (2x to 8x)** simply multiply the total fuel cost by the number of sub-spells (**2x to 8x**), with each sub-spell keeping 100% of its power.
- **Casting Apparatuses & Engraved Weapons/Tools (Wands, Staves, Grimoires, Focuses & Spellblades)**:
  - **Mandatory Socketing & Universal Tier Support**: Spellgems cannot be cast raw in-hand; they must be socketed into an Apparatus or engraved into a weapon/tool. Any Apparatus can cast any Tier 1–10 Spellgem, while higher-tier Apparatus materials increase the number of Spellgem slots and grant better fuel efficiency.
  - **5-Class Lineup**:
    - **Engraved Weapon / Tool (1 Engraved Gem)**: Mainhand sword or tool with a Spellgem engraved into it at the Arcane Scribing Table (where it can also be safely swapped or removed later). Casts on **Right-Click** and gives a **+15% boost** to Touch/Strike or Utility spells.
    - **Arcanum Focus (1–2 Gems)**: Compact starter focus with balanced `1.0x` stats.
    - **Wand (2–4 Gems)**: Fast-cast focus with **-25% spell cooldown** and quick projectile draw.
    - **Staff (4–6 Gems)**: Heavy channeling focus with **+20% Range & Potency** and **-25% fuel drain** on continuous Beams and Auras.
    - **Grimoire (8–12 Gems)**: Master spellbook holding up to 12 spells at once with a **-15% base fuel cost discount**.
  - **Workstation vs. In-Field Socketing & Spell Switching**:
    - **Safe Workstation Swapping**: Swapping Spellgems at a **Runic Anvil** or **Arcane Scribing Table** is 100% safe with a **0-second lockout** and **0% backfire chance**.
    - **Emergency In-Field Swapping**: Swapping a Spellgem in the field puts a **60-second lockout period** on the newly socketed gem AND gives its **very first cast after the 60-second lockout ends a 45% chance of backfiring**!
    - **Switching Active Spells**: Players switch between already-socketed spells instantly using either a **Radial Menu hotkey** or **Sneak + Scroll Wheel**.
- **Entropic Codex Spell Research, Starter Spells & 3-Mode Mutation Config**:
  - **Starter Spells**: Players start knowing one basic non-mutated spell per discipline (**Magic Missile** for Regular combat damage, **Basic Break** for mining, **Basic Wisp** for light, plus basic **Ward** and **Heal**).
  - **Two-Step Node Unlock (Cast $\rightarrow$ Research Bench)**: Unresearched nodes appear as world-randomized **Cryptic / Trial Glyphs** at the Scribing Table. Casting a spell with an unresearched node **1 time** in the world marks it as **Observed**, after which the player researches it at the **Research Bench** (using **Scribing Ink + Paper/Parchment + a small Materia/Essence sample** and a short timer) to permanently unlock it in the **Entropic Codex**.
  - **3-Mode Server Config for Elemental Mutations**: Server config supports 3 ways to unlock elemental spell mutations (**Mode 1**: cast each Element + Node combo to research its mutation; **Mode 2 [Default]**: researching a node at the Research Bench automatically unlocks its mutations for all Materia types the player knows; **Mode 3**: unlock mutations by tier via Materia Family Mastery nodes). Includes an initial-join chat notice for OP players explaining that the setting defaults to Mode 2 and can be changed (or have the notice turned off) in the config.
  - Synchronized all updates to the master spellcrafting guide and the Obsidian OKF Wiki.

## Build 000-1-26-308

- **Physical Spell Creation Pipeline (Blank Spellgems, Arcane Scribing Table, Spell Parchments & Magic Circle Ritual)**:
  - **Universal Blank Spellgems & Multi-Path Recipes**: A single universal **Blank Spellgem** item can hold any modular spell (from Single Tier 1 up to Octuple Tier 10). Players can craft Blank Spellgems through different progression paths, including **condensing starlight directly onto Glass**, or **combining Quartz + Materia/Essence + Amethyst and growing it submerged in Water for 10 minutes** (similar to growing Fluix crystals in *Applied Energistics 2*).
  - **Step 1 — Arcane Scribing Table & Spell Parchments**:
    - Players wire their unlocked Prefix, Flow, Form, and Core Effect nodes visually inside the **Arcane Scribing Table GUI** and pre-tag which **Materia Type** goes into each sub-spell slot (Slots 1–8).
    - Includes a live **Materia Cost, Cooldown & Preview** readout and custom **Spell Naming & Tooltip Lore** editing.
    - Consumes **Blank Parchment + Scribing Ink** to print a **Spell Parchment** blueprint.
    - **Research Locks**: Anyone can use a shared **Spell Parchment** at a Magic Circle to craft a Spellgem, **except** they cannot inspect, edit, or duplicate the parchment's nodes at the Scribing Table unless they have personally researched all of those requirements. Likewise, an existing non-Unique Spellgem can be placed into the Scribing Table to inspect its blueprint or wipe/recycle it back into a Blank Spellgem **only if** the player knows all of its spell requirements.
  - **Step 2 — Magic Circle Infusion & 8-Block Orbis Cell Fueling**:
    - Any size or tier of Magic Circle can infuse any Spellgem (Tiers 1–10). Placing the **Blank Spellgem** and **Spell Parchment** into the circle **consumes the Spell Parchment** and permanently locks the Spellgem's **Potency Tier (T1–T10)** based on the **Materia Stage** supplied.
    - The circle automatically pulls the pre-tagged Materia for each sub-spell slot from **Orbis Cells (of any tier) placed within 8 blocks of the Magic Circle**, **Materia Ampoules/Phials in Marble Ritual Bowls**, or **linked Materia Jars/Vessels**.
  - **Under-Supplied Ritual Hazard (Flawed Infusion Roll with 25% Compound Defect Chance)**:
    - If a Magic Circle is activated with less Materia than the spell requires, the Spellgem still binds, but rolls permanent defects at creation: either **Unstable Casting** (spell behaves erratically or backfires when cast), a **Randomly Crippled Spell Part** (one random stat such as Range, Power, Duration, Area, or a sub-spell slot is permanently severely weakened), or a **25% chance of getting BOTH negatives simultaneously**!
  - Synchronized all updates to the master spellcrafting guide and the Obsidian OKF Wiki.

## Build 000-1-26-289

- **Utility Spellcrafting Overhaul & Universal Autocast Pedestal**:
  - **Delivery Shapes & 57-Element Visuals**: Utility spells are primarily cast as **Beams**, **Radial Bursts**, and **Glyphs (Magic Circles)** (plus Self-Cast for personal mobility/flight), and share the exact visual styles and color palettes of their Combat counterparts across all 57 Materia types.
  - **10-Tier Area & Reach Scaling**: Aligned all 10 spell tiers with the canonical 10-Stage Materia Cycle (`Raw Essence` at Tier 1 up to `Materia Liminalia` at Tier 10):
    - **Beam Reach**: Scales from **8 blocks (Tier 1)** up to **48 blocks (Tier 10)**.
    - **Burst & Glyph Volume**: Scales from **$5\times 5\times 5$ (Tier 1)** up to **$53\times 53\times 53$ (Tier 10)**.
    - **Construction Plane & Dome Size**: Scales from **$5\times 5$ (Tier 1)** up to **$41\times 41$ (Tier 10)**.
  - **Universal Autocast Pedestal**: Added full specifications for the **Autocast Pedestal**, which can hold and timer-cast **any Spellgem (Utility, Combat, or Misc)** as an automated work station or base defense turret. Players connect an **Orbis Cell** for Materia fuel, right-click the pedestal to adjust its **Timer Slider**, and link chests or machines by **sneak-right-clicking** them with an **Arcanum Focus, Wand, Staff, or Grimoire**.
  - **Branch 1 — Excavation & Terraforming**:
    - *Beam* acts as a single-block precision laser drill (or scaled bore); *Burst* acts as a 360-degree surface ore stripper; *Glyph* acts as a delayed blasting charge that **detonates after 3 seconds** to clear rock.
    - Includes 4 sub-modes (Standard Bore, Selective Ore Vein Miner, Horizon Land Flattener, and Timber Harvester) and 4 underground safety features (Liquid Pocket Sealing, Gravity Block Support, Whitelist/Blacklist Filtering, and Auto-Torch Placement every 8 blocks).
  - **Branch 2 — Transmutation, Processing & Agriculture**:
    - Enforced strict **Chest Immunity**: Transmutation spells never break, alter, or smelt items inside chests; they only process placed blocks and floating dropped item stacks in the world.
    - Added 3 Farming & Ranching sub-modes (Smart Crop Harvester & Replanter, Gentle Rancher for shearing/milking/breeding livestock, and Orchard & Berry Picker).
    - **Tier 5+ Utility Magic Circles** now persist indefinitely as permanent automation nodes when linked to a Materia Jar, Orbis Cell pedestal, or fed ambient Materia.
  - **Branch 3 — Logistics & Retrieval**:
    - Added Magnetic Tractor Beams, 360-Degree Loot Vacuums, Hopper/Courier Collection Pads, and direct **Inventory-to-Inventory Transfers** via linked **Autocast Pedestals** without needing a Glyph.
  - **Branch 4 — Mobility & Navigation**:
    - *Beam* features two modes: a momentum-based **Swinging Grappling Hook** and a smooth **Zipline Tether** (**Sneak to pause/stop in mid-air**, **Jump to leave the zipline**).
    - *Self-Cast Flight* manifests an **Elytra-like magical wing construct** on the player's back that dynamically changes colors to match the Materia type used, unlocking **Glide at Tier 1**, **Partial Flight / Hover at Tier 2**, and **Full 3D Flight at Tier 3+**.
  - **Branch 5 — Construction & Shaping**:
    - Added Linear Bridge/Pillar Beams, Radial Plane/Dome Builders ($5\times 5$ to $41\times 41$), Architectural Blueprint Circles, In-Place Block Swappers, and 60-second Spectral Scaffolding.
    - Construction dimensions are strictly tier-based, except **Earth-lineage Materia expands Construction range by +50%** (up to 72 blocks at Tier 10).
- **Misc Spellcrafting Overhaul (9 Branches, 5 Delivery Shapes & Dynamic Lights Compatibility)**:
  - **5 Delivery Shapes, 10-Tier Scaling & 57-Element Visuals**: Misc spells can be cast as **Self-Cast/Aura**, **Projectile**, **Beam**, **Radial Burst**, or **Glyph (Magic Circle)** (including on **Autocast Pedestals**). All ranges, durations, and area sizes scale strictly across **Tiers 1–10**, while the **57 Materia types** customize the colors, particles, and shader styles of the spells.
  - **Branch 3.1 — Sensory Detection & Starlight Pathfinding**: Releases drifting starlight trail particles through solid rock (from **32 blocks at Tier 1** up to **256 blocks at Tier 10**) to guide players toward **Specific Ore Veins** (matched to offhand or filter), **Natural Materia Nodes & Rare Materia Crystals**, or **Underground Structures, Geodes, Dungeons & Trial Chambers**.
  - **Branch 3.2 — Multi-Mode Illumination**:
    - **Orbiting Starlight Wisp**: Uses lag-free shader/bloom glow by default, and automatically acts as a true mobile light source if the player has a **Dynamic Lights mod** installed!
    - Also includes **Permanent Light Blocks/Runes** (adjustable brightness Level 7–15), **Timed Scouting Flares** (30–60s arcing cave flares), and **Luminescent Breadcrumb Trails** (drops glowing star-dots behind you in caves that fade after 10 minutes).
  - **Branch 3.3 — Passive Acrobatic & Surface Buffs**: Supports both **Continuous Passive Toggle** and **Armor Buffer Charge** for **Feather Fall / Soft Landing** and **Water Walking & Lava Striding**.
  - **Branch 3.4 — Celestial Observation & Atmospheric Control**: Includes the **Cloud Cleanser** (clears storm clouds in a 16 to 128-block cylinder), **Solar/Lunar Glare Filter ("Eclipse Lens")** so players can see stars and constellations clearly during bright daytime, and **Localized Weather Domes** ($5\times 5\times 5$ to $53\times 53\times 53$ harmless snow, cherry petals, aurora glow, or sunbeams).
  - **Branch 3.5 — Auditory Lures & Sculk Silence**: Includes the **Phantom Vibration Lure** (8 to 48 blocks to distract Sculk Sensors, Wardens, and un-alerted/blind mobs) and **Zone of Silence / Acoustic Muffler** ($5\times 5\times 5$ to $53\times 53\times 53$ soundproof aura or magic circle that hides footsteps, mining, and chest noises from Sculk and Wardens).
  - **Branch 3.6 — Vanity, Pyrotechnics, Trails & Holograms**: Includes **Zodiac Firework Pyrotechnics**, **Chromatic Footstep/Ribbon Trails**, and **3D Illusory Hologram / Diorama Projectors**.
  - **Branch 3.7 — Diagnostic Materia Meter & Inspector**: Combines the **Analog Crosshair Needle Gauge** (detecting the 5 Materia sources and rare single-harvest Materia Crystals) with a **Machine & Pipe Inspector** (showing flow rate, pressure, purity tier, and bottlenecks) and a **Mob/Player Aura Readout** (showing exact HP, armor, active hexes/buffs, and elemental weaknesses).
  - **Branch 3.8 — Identity, Text Sigils & Waypoints**: Includes **Permanent Hovering Text Sigils** for labeling chests/machines and **Directional Waypoint Beacons** visible through walls (32 to 256 blocks).
  - **Branch 3.9 — Gravity Manipulation**: Scales from **$5\times 5\times 5$ (Tier 1)** up to **$53\times 53\times 53$ (Tier 10)** across **Zero-G Weightless Bubbles**, **Inverted Gravity Ceiling-Walking**, **Heavy-G Grounding/Anti-Jump Wells**, and the **Personal Gravity Dial** (25% Lunar, 100% Standard, 250% Heavy Anchor).
  - Synchronized all updates to the master spellcrafting guide and the Obsidian OKF Wiki.

## Build 000-1-26-286

- **Elemental Spellcrafting Engine (Global Hex Overhaul, Player Hex Ecology, Vital Fusions, Ethereal & Paradox Fusions: Eclipse through Barrow, Tier 3 Trinity Fusions: Pyre through Oblivion, Chimera Byproduct, Tier 4 Apex Fusions: Aether, Entropic, Celestial, Prismatic, Tier 5 Eschaton: Empyrean, Cataclysm & Terminus & Tier 6 Singularity: Eschaton, Apotheosis & Entropica)**:
  - Systematically overhauled all 30 previously documented Materia Afflictions/Hexes across Base Types #1–12 and Fusions #13–30 to strictly enforce the Persistent Hex standard: curses persist indefinitely on standard targets and cannot be cured by drinking milk. Death contagion only spreads if the victim dies within the first few seconds of being cursed or while the spell is actively channeled. Bosses are the only resistant mobs, capped at a 30-second affliction timer with a 10-second temporary immunity window once a hex expires. Hexes can only be cleared by target death, waiting out boss timers, dedicated Restoration/Vitae magic, elemental counter-reactions, or defeating the caster.
  - Introduced the **Player Hex Ecology Overhaul (Spawn-Lure Beacons & Monster Empowerment)**: When a player is afflicted with a hex, surrounding monsters react aggressively based on the element:
    - **7 Spawn-Lure Beacons**: Blood (#22), Undead (#9), Void (#10), Barrow (#38), Miasma (#45), Oblivion (#47), and Entropic (#49) act as environmental beacons that accelerate hostile monster spawn rates around the afflicted player by +100% to +200% within a 32-block radius.
    - **9 Monster Empowerment Vectors**: Umbral (#8), Arid (#11), Blight (#26), Soulfire (#28), Abyss (#33), Aegis (#34), Mirage (#36), Chimera (Classic), and Aether (#48) make surrounding monsters deal extra damage, bypass player armor, gain lifesteal, score extra critical hits, or gain elemental weapon buffs against the afflicted player.
  - Finalized all 9 spell forms, flow modifiers, and core effects for **Blood (#22)**, **Aura (#23)**, **Amber (#24)**, **Eclipse (#25)**, **Blight (#26)**, **Astral (#27)**, **Soulfire (#28)**, **Vapor (#29)**, **Null_R (#30)**, **Null_U (#31)**, **Dawn (#32)**, **Abyss (#33)**, **Aegis (#34)**, **Aurora (#35)**, **Mirage (#36)**, **Wraith (#37)**, and **Barrow (#38)**, concluding the Vital Fusions branch and completing fourteen major Ethereal & Paradox Fusions:

    - **Aura (#23)**: Added piercing spiritual gale lances that drag enemies in vortexes and deflect arrows, continuous pranic jetstream tether beams that repel hostiles and act as a vertical jetpack lifter, spiritual vacuum implosions that pull mobs and gather items, chakra disruptor palm strikes that silence mob abilities and siphon fuel, a meditative chakra respiration shroud granting 20% fuel discount and full drowning/suffocation immunity, chakra disruption glyph traps, wide pranic gale deluge cones, aerial spiritual gale barrages launching enemies 10 blocks upward, a floating pranic spiritualist summon triad (Zephyr-Monk, Chakra Respiration Spire, and sacrificial Spirit-Vane Effigy), pranic slipstream chains, silent slipstream blinks leaving vacuum pops, aerated purifying transmutations that clear potion clouds and cobwebs, and a persistent chakra severance hex that completely silences mob abilities and doubles knockback taken.

## Build 000-1-26-253

- **Forcefield & Firmament Barriers**:
  - Added paper-thin, iridescent forcefield barriers that float in mid-air without occupying block space (you can place and build blocks freely through them).
  - Built a pluggable modular shape system supporting 6 shapes: flat rectangular walls, circular discs, hemispherical domes, spherical bubbles, cylindrical columns, and arbitrary convex polygons.
  - Fully impenetrable with continuous swept collision detection—running, jumping, falling, or shooting fast arrows and fireballs into them never clips through, and bounces them cleanly away from the side they approached.
  - Fast flight-path tracking intercepts high-speed arrows, tridents, and fireballs in mid-air and reflects them along realistic bounce angles.
  - If a path crosses multiple shields, the game sorts them by distance and bounces off the closest shield first without skipping past.
  - Shimmers like real celestial thin films with rainbow light effects, edge glows, and expanding wave ripples that grow larger the faster an object hits the shield.
  - Added One-Way Directional Valve mode: walking through the front face lets you pass through freely, while approaching from behind acts as a solid elastic bounce wall, with drifting starlight arrows showing the open direction.
  - Added Redstone and Materia switchability: barriers can be turned on or off using redstone signals (switches, buttons, wire), turning dormant, transparent, and collision-free when powered down.
  - Boss arena barriers are invincible against survival player dismantling while the boss lives, and instantly dissolve in a brilliant starlight and musical chime fanfare when the boss is defeated.
  - Added 6 custom procedural themes for the Apex Predators of Astral Materia: The Star Eater (black hole void with crimson tendrils), The Void Leviathan (abyssal azure tidal sheen), The Entropic Chimera (shifting fire/frost/lightning flux), The Defiler of Symmetries (non-Euclidean amethyst facets), The Unmaker of Forms (acidic bubbling chartreuse sludge), and The Silencer of Echoes (light-absorbing smoky obsidian).
  - Added custom Materia color tinting so barriers can be tinted with dyes or crystals while preserving beautiful shifting highlights. Right-clicking with a wet sponge or water bottle resets the color to standard rainbow.
  - Supports permission filters to block everyone, only mobs, only players, hostile mobs, or flying projectiles.
  - **Interior Boundary Bounce**: Entities trying to leave a spherical bubble, dome, or cylinder from the inside are now detected on the inner wall and flung back towards the center, keeping contained creatures securely inside.
  - **High-Energy Fling Impulses**: Contact with barriers now applies a strong elastic bounce with an upward arc on vertical walls, preventing entities from sticking to ground friction and launching them airborne. Deflected projectiles reverse flight heading cleanly.
  - **Unobstructed Block & Item Interactions**: Fixed barriers intercepting crosshair clicks. You can now place blocks, open chests, use furnaces, mine, and use items freely inside and around barriers; the barrier only registers clicks when holding the Firmament Weaver or dyes and aiming directly at the shield membrane.

- **Barrier Hardening & Security Defenses**:
  - Fixed shield boundary math so flying or jumping upward into dome shields and diagonal cylinder walls bounces you correctly instead of passing through.
  - Blocked stationary and slow-moving creatures on the reverse side of one-way shields so they can never leak or squeeze through backwards.
  - Protected bounce velocity calculations against invalid or negative values so players and mobs never get pinned or stuck in a jitter loop.
  - Added strict network safeguards to stop bad data packets with broken numbers or oversized player lists from disabling barrier collision.
  - Reinforced boss battle arenas so shields only dissolve when the real boss is defeated, not if friendly helpers or players fall in battle.
  - Added a delay buffer to redstone switches so rapid clock pulses cannot flicker a shield off for a split second to let fast entities slip through.
  - Locked in clean, reliable edge-fusing snapping when using the Firmament Weaver to connect shield walls together seamlessly.

- **Firmament Weaver Tool & Hologram Utilities**:
  - **Two-Point Drag & Snap ("Anchor & Stretch")**: Click once on any block or surface to drop a glowing anchor point (Point A). As you move, a starlight guide line and live preview box stretch out to your crosshair (Point B). Clicking again locks in the placement and spans a flat barrier wall between the two points to seal doorways and hallways. Crouching and right-clicking cancels the active anchor.
  - **Shift + Mouse Wheel Shape Cycling**: Sneak and scroll your mouse wheel up or down while holding the Firmament Weaver to cycle forward and backward through all 6 barrier shapes. The in-world hologram updates instantly with audio feedback and syncs across the network, while regular hotbar slot scrolling is protected.
  - **Seamless Edge-Fusing Snapping**: Barriers now automatically snap cleanly to nearby existing barriers within half a block (0.5m). Easily snap barriers flush end-to-end to build long walls, snap at 90-degree corners, snap at 45-degree angles, or stack them vertically on top of each other with zero gaps.
  - **In-World Holographic Placement Preview**: Holding the Firmament Weaver projects a real-time starlight hologram showing the exact shape, size, and angle of the barrier before you place it. The hologram glows emerald green when snapping into place against another barrier, starlight cyan for standard placement, and crimson red if obstructed or out of reach.
  - **Barrier Telemetry HUD**: Pointing the Firmament Weaver at any barrier within 16 blocks brings up a celestial telemetry card on screen. It displays the creator's name, shield shape and exact dimensions, permission filter, bounce elasticity, one-way flow direction, redstone mode, and active status.
  - **Shape Cycling & Left-Click Quick Dispel**: Sneak and right-click or mouse scroll to cycle barrier shapes in your hand. Left-clicking an existing barrier you own instantly dispels it with a gentle, crisp bubble pop sound effect and subtle starlight puff, decoupled from boss battle victory fanfares. Right-clicking is safely reserved for tinting and configurations, preventing accidental deletion.
  - **Easy 2D Shield Targeting & Crosshair Picking**: Fixed paper-thin 2D barrier walls and discs being hard to aim at from the side or oblique angles. They now have a generous targeting zone so you can look at them or their edges from any angle and easily click to configure or dispel them.
  - **Anchor Expiration & 32m Range Capping**: Drag-and-drop anchor points are now strictly capped to 32 blocks (matching the configuration slider). Walking more than 32 blocks away, changing dimensions, or right-clicking into the air automatically cancels the anchor point, preventing giant preview planes from stretching across the world and following the player.
  - **Accidental Placement Protection**: Right-clicking empty air no longer spawns an unwanted shield wall right in front of your face; instead, you get a helpful reminder to click a block to anchor Point A or sneak to cycle shapes.
  - **Shift + Left-Click Proximity Dispel**: Sneaking and left-clicking with the Firmament Weaver automatically dispels the nearest shield you own within 5 blocks, eliminating the need to aim with pixel precision.
  - **Luminous Edge Rims**: Flat walls, discs, and polygons now feature soft glowing starlight borders along all perimeter edges, ensuring they stay clearly visible from the side rather than disappearing into a 1-pixel glitch line.
  - **Materia Crystal & Dye Tinting**: Right-clicking a barrier with vanilla dyes or Materia crystals (Astral, Aeterium, Ignisite, Mortisite) tints the barrier while preserving its iridescent shine. Right-clicking with a wet sponge or water bottle cleans the barrier and resets it back to normal.

- **Creator Configuration Screen & Network Synchronization**:
  - Sneak and right-click any forcefield barrier you own with the Firmament Weaver to open the celestial creator configuration screen.
  - Interactive shape and permission filter selectors let you cycle between all 6 barrier shapes and 5 entity filter modes directly from the menu.
  - Smooth stepped sliders let you customize barrier width, height, and radius from 1 to 32 blocks (in half-block steps), and dial bounce springiness from soft cushion to high-velocity trampoline.
  - Added one-way directional valve toggles and redstone switch mode buttons right inside the interface.
  - Built-in player whitelist manager allows adding or removing permitted player usernames with text input and quick-delete so friends can walk through freely.
  - Saves and applies settings instantly across the server with built-in security checks so only the shield's creator or creative players can modify them, while protecting boss arena shields against tampering.

- **Graviton Bouncepad Block**:
  - Added a celestial spring bouncepad that launches players and mobs upward relative to whatever downward gravity they currently feel.
  - Works consistently whether you are on normal floors, upside down on ceilings (launching downward into the room), walking around curved asteroids with a Gravity Center, or wall-running.
  - Wall-mounted bouncepads launch players horizontally across chasms, and zero-gravity pads launch directly outward from the pad face.
  - Smooth analogue redstone scaling: redstone power smoothly dials launch velocity from a gentle hop up to a massive 28-block launch.
  - Added total landing fall damage protection: entities launched by bouncepads land safely with zero fall damage.
  - Added mechanical recoil timing so pads compress cleanly without rapid-fire re-triggering or physics pinning.
  - Features animated pneumatic piston recoil, metallic launch sounds, and glowing sparkle bursts.

- **Survival Crafting Recipes & Entropic Codex Progression**:
  - Added shaped crafting recipes for the Firmament Weaver (using Arcanite ingots or End rods, Astral Crystals, and Starlight Lenses) and Graviton Bouncepad (using Arcanite or Resonite plates, Piston, and Graviton Core).
  - Integrated research entries for the Firmament Weaver (under Magic) and Graviton Bouncepad (under Machinery) into the in-game Entropic Codex with detailed guides, prerequisites, and lore.
  - Added full in-game translations (`en_us.json`) for all barrier settings, telemetry HUD readouts, bouncepad tooltips, and codex entries.

- **Gravity & Barrier Debug Commands**:
  - Added `/entropica gravity` commands to view, set, reset, or invert personal gravity and spawn test fields, complete with full tab auto-completion for gravity modes (`zero_g`, `lunar`, `inversion`, `singularity`, `grav_lift`, `repulsor`, `tidal_pulse`).
  - Added `/entropica barrier` commands to spawn barriers of any shape, erect boss arenas, view barrier stats, and test bounce velocities, complete with full tab auto-completion for shapes (`planar_quad`, `circular_disc`, `hemispherical_dome`, `spherical_bubble`, `cylinder`, `convex_polygon`), filters (`all_entities`, `mobs_only`, `players_only`, `hostile_mobs`, `projectiles`), and themes (`standard`, `star_eater`, `void_leviathan`, `entropic_chimera`, `defiler_of_symmetries`, `unmaker_of_forms`, `silencer_of_echoes`).
  - Fixed barrier entity hitboxes and swept collision checks across all sizes so mobs and players cannot walk through or tunnel into barrier walls.
  - Implemented high-energy kinetic fling back: entities contacting a barrier are now powerfully launched backward with an upward arc instead of sticking to ground friction, sailing 4 to 6 blocks through the air.
  - Creator passage rule: the barrier creator is now flung back like everyone else unless sneaking while holding the Firmament Weaver, allowing builders to test barriers and bounce off them naturally.
  - Projectile deflection: incoming arrows, tridents, and fireballs are now cleanly deflected in mid-air and shot backward away from the shield without sticking into the barrier or vanishing.
  - Server-side velocity synchronization: teleportation and motion packets are instantly broadcast to players and mobs upon collision, guaranteeing punchy, responsive rebounds on both server and client.

- **Gravity Center Core Improvements**:
  - The Gravity Center now completely overrides standard downward gravity within its radius, so its center is the only direction of gravity.
  - Calibrated the inward pull strength and fixed player jumping so you can jump at full, normal height even when right next to or on top of the core.
  - Stabilized player orientation around the core with a smooth transition zone, preventing rapid back-and-forth gravity flipping and camera jitter.
  - Automatically resets player gravity back to normal when stepping outside the active field or breaking the core.

- **Graviton Soles Wall Walking & Smooth Rotation**:
  - Removed downward sliding when running horizontally along walls with Graviton Soles equipped.
  - The camera now smoothly tilts 90 degrees when you run along walls, aligning your view with the wall surface so it feels like a floor.
  - In third-person view, your character model smoothly rotates 90 degrees against the wall face so your feet touch the wall.
  - Looking and moving up or jumping lets you climb up, while looking down or moving backward lets you climb down smoothly.

- **Illuminated Balustrade Diagonal Gap Fix**:
  - Extended the bottom stone base rails down to floor level (and down into the lower stair step on upper slanting-down sections), completely eliminating the empty air gap under the railing on stairs.

- **Astral Crystal Appearance & Hitbox Polish**:
  - Fixed the crystal facet mesh so outer walls are completely solid, brilliant, and opaque from the outside instead of appearing see-through or inverted.
  - Connected crystal rings with seamless, watertight corners to eliminate cracks and gaps along mesh seams.
  - Added dynamic, custom hitboxes for all 4 crystal growth stages (Small Bud, Medium Bud, Large Bud, and Cluster) that match their actual spires and shapes across floors, walls, and ceilings.

- **Multi-Loader Verification & Dev Deployment**:
  - Verified 0-error build across Architectury Common, Fabric, and NeoForge modules with 100% test suite pass rate.
  - Built and deployed fresh jar files to both Fabric and NeoForge dev test instances.
  - Synchronized complete OKF documentation in the Obsidian Vault.



## Build 000-1-26-252

- **Gravity Center Core (Fueled & Creative Variants)**:
  - Added an omnidirectional point-gravity block that acts like the center of gravity for mini-planets and asteroids.
  - Pulls players and creatures directly towards the block center from all 360-degree directions, allowing full spherical and cubical walking around floating structures.
  - **Directional Down Adhesion**: Players and creatures within the core's active radius experience Graviton Sole-like surface lock, total fall damage negation, and edge-wrapping strictly in its "down" direction (towards the core center).
  - **Fueled Variant**: Encased in arcanite and brass, connects directly to Vapor Pneumatic Pipes on all six sides, and consumes 1 mB of Materia Fumus vapor every 2 seconds to sustain its gravitational field. Right-clicking cycles the active radius between 8, 16, 24, 32, and 48 blocks.
  - **Creative Mode Variant**: Infinite power version for world builders and mapmakers with configurable radius up to 64 blocks without needing any fuel.
  - **Planetary Underside Inversion**: When players walk or fall below the equator of the core, gravity smoothly inverts with a 180-degree camera roll so the bottom of the asteroid becomes the new floor.
  - Features spinning brass planetary gimbal rings, an inner glowing graviton core, and a floating holographic telemetry status readout.
- **Gravitational Anchor Machine**:
  - Added a multi-mode field emitter encased in brass plating that projects wide-area gravity fields.
  - Supports 6 distinct operational modes: *Zero-G Chamber*, *Lunar Pavilion*, *Graviton Inversion*, *Singularity Well*, *Repulsor Dome*, and *Tidal Pulse*.
  - Added a floating holographic mode name billboard directly above the anchor and instant action bar feedback when right-clicking to cycle modes with any tool or empty hand.
  - Features spinning brass gimbals and a floating, mode-colored graviton core in the center.
  - Fully controllable with redstone levers and wire signals.
- **Tidal Pulse Resonator Machine**:
  - Added a dedicated standalone celestial machine block that projects rhythmic gravity tides.
  - Operates on a continuous 4-second cycle: 3 seconds of gentle low-gravity floating followed by a 1-second heavy downward gravity slam.
  - Features 4 vibrating brass tuning prongs, a glowing pulsating graviton core, and expanding energy shockwave rings.
  - Can be turned on or off by hand or locked in place using redstone levers and wire signals.
- **Grav-Lift Projector**:
  - Directional elevator fixture that projects a pneumatic travel beam up to 24 blocks.
  - Smoothly lifts or pushes players and mobs along the beam axis at 0.35 m/s without fall damage.
  - Can be oriented vertically (+Y/-Y) or horizontally.
- **Singularity Grenade**:
  - Thrown tactical projectile that anchors upon landing and opens a 4-second miniature black hole.
  - Pulls in nearby mobs, bends arrows and fireballs mid-flight, and collapses in a destructive sonic shockwave.
- **Graviton Wand**:
  - Handheld tuning rod to cycle personal gravity between Normal (100%), Moon (25%), Zero-G (0%), and Inverted (-100%).
  - Right-click any mob to float them in zero-g or slam them down with heavy crushing gravity.
- **Graviton Soles (Curios & Footwear)**:
  - Advanced pneumatic boots that activate reliably when worn in your armor boots slot, equipped in your Curios/Accessories feet slot, or held in your hands.
  - **Client-Authoritative Physics Engine**: Movement inputs now execute immediately on the client side with network packet synchronization, delivering instantaneous response without server delay or missed keypresses.
  - **Wall Sticking & Climbing**: Crouching against any wall locks your vertical position with zero slipping or sliding. Moving forward (W) or jumping (Space) climbs up the wall face at 0.26 m/s, while moving backward (S) climbs down at 0.22 m/s.
  - **Ceiling Adhesion & Smooth Roll**: Touching or climbing into ceiling blocks automatically inverts your gravity (-0.08) with a smooth 180° camera roll, letting you walk on ceilings just like regular floors.
  - **360° Block Edge Wrapping**: Walking or crouching over the edge of any platform or block smoothly wraps your footing around the corner between floor and ceiling, or grips vertical side walls to prevent falling off into the void. Transitions are accompanied by musical celestial chimes and glittering starlight particles.
  - **Dynamic Camera & Look Controls Adaptation**: When rotated upside down on ceilings or tilted near singularities, mouse look controls automatically rotate with the camera so moving the mouse right turns right on screen and moving the mouse up tilts up on screen, keeping controls natural and intuitive.
  - **100% Fall Damage Negation**: Pressurized kinetic dampeners completely absorb all impact shock, setting fall damage to zero and protecting you from falls of any height.
- **Inertial Anchor Amulet (Curios Charm)**:
  - Arcane amulet equippable in the Curios charm slot.
  - Provides total immunity against external gravity shifts, vortex suction, singularity collapse fling, and tidal crushing.
- **Curios & Accessories Gear System**:
  - Added official Curios slot definitions for `feet`, `charm`, `head`, `hands`, `belt`, and `ring` slots, complete with custom empty slot icons and built-in mod fallbacks.
  - Automatically configured default player equipment assignments so all six accessory slots are ready to use in the Curios inventory menu.
  - Graviton Soles fit into the `feet` slot and Inertial Anchor Amulets fit into the `charm` slot, freeing up standard armor slots for your armor sets.
- **System Stability & Immersion Refinements**:
  - Potion effects now cleanly restore standard gravity as soon as the effect expires or is cleared.
  - Gravity fields and projectors now stay isolated to their own dimension, preventing server desync.
  - Approaching singularity wells smoothly banks the camera without interfering with crosshair aiming.
  - Dropped items in zero-gravity chambers float and bob gently in mid-air without sinking to the floor.
  - Respiration cycles for Tidal Pulse Resonators now resume in perfect sync with physical gravity after being unpaused by redstone.
  - Redstone signals no longer overwrite manual hand activation states on Tidal Pulse Resonators.
  - Graviton Soles internal cooldown memory now automatically cleans up expired entries to conserve server resources.
  - Gravitational lensing overlays now have safety checks to prevent visual glitches at extreme or zero field-of-view camera angles.

### True Black Hole Gravitational Lensing Engine
- **Authentic 3D Black Hole Graphics**:
  - Added real astrophysical black hole geometry to Singularity Grenades and Singularity-mode Gravitational Anchors.
  - Features a pitch-black spherical event horizon that completely blocks the view of objects behind it, surrounded by a blinding white-hot photon halo ring.
  - Includes a tilted equatorial gas disk and arched light ribbons curving over the top and bottom poles where extreme gravity bends rear starlight directly toward the player.
  - Relativistic Doppler lighting makes the approaching side of the disk shine brightly in electric cyan while the receding side dims in deep celestial purple.
- **Dedicated Singularity Grenade Renderer**:
  - Thrown grenades now display the flying item in mid-air, then smoothly expand into a swirling, pulsating 3D black hole upon anchoring before snapping closed in an implosive shockwave.
- **Gravitational Anchor Singularity Display**:
  - Setting a Gravitational Anchor to Singularity mode now places the miniature 3D black hole core spinning smoothly inside the brass gimbal rings instead of a plain colored cube.
- **Screen-Space Gravitational Distortion Overlay**:
  - When looking towards a nearby singularity, the player's view dynamically distorts around the black hole's exact screen position with three concentric chromatic rings (electric cyan, caustic white, and magenta) and swirling starlight refraction rays.

### Procedural Irregular Astral Crystals & Starlight Fountain Visual Polish
- **Procedural Non-Blocky Crystal Geometry**:
  - Completely replaced Minecraft blocky cuboid shapes for Astral Crystals with custom procedural geometry inspired by the Materia Blessing.
  - Crystals now have natural, sharp, irregular crystal facets that break standard Minecraft grid lines.
  - Features asymmetric spiraling rings, fine gemstone sparkle shading, and natural mineral faceting.
- **Dynamic Growth Stages & Gemstones**:
  - **In-World Blocks**: Placed crystals grow naturally on floors, walls, and ceilings in 4 distinct irregular formations:
    - *Small Bud*: A delicate, sharp needle spire with a tiny basal spur.
    - *Medium Bud*: An angled primary crystal spire with a leaning companion spire.
    - *Large Bud*: A prominent jagged spire with two leaning satellites and a base shard.
    - *Cluster*: A grand central obelisk spire flanked by four tilted satellite spires and jagged ground shards.
  - **Inventory & Held Gem**: The Astral Crystal item now renders as a double-terminated floating gemstone with sharp top and bottom points and two tiny orbiting crystal shards.
- **Starlight Fountain Depth Sorting & Lighting Fix**:
  - Fixed a visual glitch where block models (such as the brass telescope) rendered with full-bright colors and appeared in front of the Starlight Fountain.
  - Starlight Fountain water and spray veil now properly draw in layered passes so they blend in front of background objects smoothly.
  - Telescopes and background blocks now properly use realistic ambient world and sky lighting.

### Starlight Cloth & Frictionless Starlight Carpets
- **Starlight Cloth**:
  - Added a new celestial fabric woven from Starlight Silk and Astral Crystal Thread.
  - Features an intricate midnight-blue starry weave with constellation designs.
  - Used for crafting and weaves directly into high-velocity carpets.
- **Starlight Carpet & Infinite Speed Fix**:
  - Carpet-style floor covering that gives players and mobs a celestial speed boost and smooth gliding physics.
  - Fixed an infinite speed bug caused by compounding velocity multiplication; replaced with a controlled celestial speed boost (Speed II) that provides fast movement without runaway acceleration or losing steering control.
  - Emits soft starlight illumination (light level 8), ambient twinkling sparks, and sparkling starlight footprints underfoot.
  - Fully reversible: 2 cloth makes 3 carpet, and 3 carpet can be unraveled back into 2 cloth.

Beams now remain completely visible regardless of camera angle or facing direction when the base block is off screen.

Intersecting creatures suffer Materia toxicity and damage, and destination blocks (such as pedestals, collectors, and receivers) are irradiated.

  - Upgraded all optical beams (Refractive Lens, Secondary Lens, Astral Collector, Beam Splitter Prism, and Optic Transmitter) to dynamically tint their core beam and outer glowing aura to match the active Materia type, including smooth chromatic cycling for multi-color Materia.

  - Upgraded the distributor to measure and store pure **Starlight Flux** directly from incoming collector beams, showing accurate flux levels on right-click.
- **Supernova Remnant Essence Types**:
  - Fixed supernova remnants in `StellarRemnantType` so they only select from high-tier complete compound essences (e.g. Void, Cataclysm, Static, Lightning, Radiant, Apotheosis, Pyre, Empyrean), strictly eliminating conceptual fragment essences (Singularity, Volt, Kinetic, Density, Photon).
  - Added runtime safety checks guaranteeing that fragment essences are never assigned to supernova remnants.
- **Modular Lamp Posts**:
  - Added the **Lamp Post** family with 4 distinct material variants: **Iron**, **Brass**, **Steel**, and **Essence Glass**.
  - **Zero-Gap Baseplate**: The base block features a full 16×16×1 flush bottom plate that completely conceals underlying optic fiber conduits and floor holes with zero visible gaps.
  - **Dynamic 1-to-6 Block Height & Seamless Shaft Stacking**:
    - Lamp posts can now be extended up to **6 blocks tall** (enforcing a hard cap at 6 high).
    - Features seamless 6×6 shaft connections with ornamental joint bands across intermediate blocks, stepping gracefully from a broad base plinth up to a slender neck, arm collar, and crown finial at any height from 1 to 6 blocks.
  - **Pronounced 4:1 Vertical Tapering**:
    - Re-engineered the column shaft geometry across 6 dynamic tiers (`single`, `bottom`, `lower_middle`, `middle`, `upper_middle`, `top`).
    - Creates a striking architectural taper from a broad 16-pixel plinth base down to a slender 3-pixel neck and 2-pixel spire finial at the top.
  - **Translucent Essence Glass & Texture Overhaul**:
    - Enabled true alpha translucency on `glass_lamp_post` across all models and render layers, letting light, terrain, and blocks shine through the crystal glass.
    - Overhauled all 4 material textures with higher contrast and depth: authentic gunmetal wrought-iron with stippled grit and bevels, rich polished brass with golden sheen, industrial cold-rolled steel with brushed grain, and crystalline glass with etched facet borders and refraction glints.
  - **Astral Collector Hand Extraction & mFum Units**:
    - Astral Collectors now measure stored Materia strictly in **mFum** (milli-fume) rather than mB, where **8 mFum = 1 Small Essence**.
    - Players can now extract stored Materia directly by hand: clicking with an empty hand condenses 8 mFum into 1 Small Essence orb of the collector's active essence type.
    - Sneak right-clicking with an empty hand bulk-condenses up to a full stack (up to 64 essences) at once.
    - Sneak right-clicking with a socketed crystal extracts the crystal safely.
  - **Lens Beam Focal Alignment on Astral Collectors**:
    - Beams from Refractive and Secondary Astral Lenses now cut off precisely at the Astral Collector's floating crystal focal point ($Y = 1.20$), meeting the exact spot where the collector's outgoing beam begins. Eliminates beam clipping, floor penetration, and passing through the crystal.
  - **Continuous Multi-Tier Lamp Post Tapering**:
    - Re-sculpted the 3D models across all tiers (`single`, `bottom`, `lower_middle`, `middle`, `upper_middle`, `top`) so that every individual tier visibly steps inward as it rises ($16 \rightarrow 12 \rightarrow 10 \rightarrow 9 \rightarrow 8 \rightarrow 7 \rightarrow 6 \rightarrow 5 \rightarrow 4 \rightarrow 3$).
    - Added automated column synchronisation so placing or breaking any lamp post in a column immediately updates all connected blocks to maintain a continuous, graceful taper from base to finial.
  - **Optic Cables with Internal Starlight Flux Rendering**:
    - Pure Optic Fiber cables now feature an active inner starlight core that renders through their observation jacket.
    - When carrying starlight, the internal core brightly illuminates and pulses in the exact chromatic color of the Materia essence being transported across the network.
  - **Surgical Arm De-Toggling & Breaking**:
    - Players can now de-toggle individual arms without breaking the entire lamp post!
    - **Punching / Left-Click**: Punching an arm with an empty hand or tool pops off only that specific attachment (`BULB` drops the bulb and keeps the bracket; `CABLE` drops the fiber; `ARM` retracts the bracket) while leaving the post standing. Punching the center column mines the post normally.
    - **Right-Click**: Clicking an arm with an empty hand now cycles and pops off the bulb or cable safely and returns the item.

## Build 000-1-26-237

### Astral Mirror Fixes
- **Constellation Line Rendering**: Fixed stretched and distorted lines in the Astral Mirror reflections by ensuring constellation connections are only drawn between stars on the same reflection plane.

### Mob Polish & Flight Refinements
- **Aurorafowl Wing Membranes & Ground Stance**: Wings and flight membranes now fold vertically flush and parallel against the bird's flanks with dedicated polygon spacing to prevent any Z-fighting or clipping.
- **Flight & Ground Transitions**: The bird stays in flight until it actually nears the ground (within 2-3 blocks) and descends, avoiding any premature mid-air ground transitions, and unfolds its wings upon takeoff.
- **Walking Pose Stability**: Fixed a glitch where walking over block edges caused brief single-frame flashes of the flight pose.
- **Sinusoidal Air Gliding**: Airborne idling features smooth wave oscillations across the wings and wingtips as the bird rides thermals in lazy circles.
- **Climbing Flap Animation**: The bird actively flaps its wings whenever it climbs to gain height or sprints, smoothly gliding once cruising altitude is reached.
- **Fall Damage Immunity**: Aurorafowl is fully protected against fall damage.
- **Geyser Wiggle-Worm Scale & Texture**: Restored the original high-resolution volcanic texture and scaled the worm to 1.75x with an expanded hitbox to reflect its true leviathan size in-game.

### Visual FX & Particles
- **Shadow Slash Particle (`shadow_slash`)**: Added custom emissive violet/obsidian claw swipe attack particle for Abyss/Nighthaunt predators with clean alpha transparency, dynamic rotation, and fading strike expansion.

## Build 000-1-26-236

### Astral Multiblocks & Starlight Networks
- **Astral Collection Altar (5x5x3)**: Added a new multiblock collector that harnesses starlight through a floating crystal. It can store celestial flux and automatically beam energy to nearby altars and optical lenses.
- **Collector Chaining & Cannibalization**: Multiple collection altars can now link together in sequence to combine their power into stronger starlight beams without creating infinite feedback loops.
- **Master Astral Altar Network (11x11x7)**: Added the grand Tier 3 altar with 12 ritual pedestals and 4 tall corner towers. When powered by collection beams, it casts a multi-colored rainbow light cascade over the ritual matrix and dramatically speeds up crystal growth and crafting.

- **Seamless Mirror Pools**: Fixed visual seams and star clipping across multi-block mirror floors and walls.
